# Complete matched component results — 12 November 2026

See the [main report](tissue-component-attribution-2026-11-12.md) for definitions and restrictions. Values below are sample estimates. ECE is ten-bin runner-weighted error. Each component variant uses exactly its cohort's same races and runners. Comparisons are local to their frozen baseline. Absolute calibration and strike intervals, full priced expectations, largest-winning-bet identities, paired Brier/strike/ROI intervals and headline changed-winner records are also in the evidence JSON. No weights were fitted.
